Appointment adds a temporal function, so the same sign can orient movement or announce a due moment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Succession, Gait, and Race Order","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_518079a4cfb9b01b8cde","text":"Succession supplies order, gait supplies the repeated motion that maintains it, and the second horse makes relative position explicit. Swaying and trotting are contrasting realizations of patterned advance.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Spirit and Apparition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5429d2b9e5ecac24a011","text":"`ر ء ي` 96:7, 96:9, 96:11, 96:13-14; `ء ن س` 96:2, 96:5-6; `ط غ ي` 96:6","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Territorial Partition and Boundary Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_54d5943a927287ab4c34","text":"Measurement establishes extent, cutting creates the tract, the mark distinguishes it, and place gives the partition a stable spatial identity.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_000531/B012","root_001210/B010","root_001486/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Showing, Detecting, and Verifying","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_58c081f4f05322e32ae2","text":"deliberate showing `ر ء ي:B012/m01`; sensory detection `ء ن س:B002/m01`; detention for verification `ق ر ء:B010/m01`; manifest appearance `ن د و:B008/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Care, Guarantee, and Protective Support","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_58c9c378d429a8eeadf0","text":"Surety assigns responsibility, nurture supplies continued care, adequacy defines the required outcome, and protection preserves the supported person from loss.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Flags, Marks, and Guiding Landmarks","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_59c8ba64f5778ebea137","text":"Something hidden or unknown becomes apprehensible through instruction, perception, inquiry, indication, or controlled disclosure.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Proportion, Appearance, and Reflection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5bc9df133ce4b663b604","text":"`خ ل ق` 96:1-2; `ء م ر` 96:12 (أَمَرَ); `ع ل م` 96:4-5, 96:14 (عَلَّمَ، يَعْلَمْ)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Lung Ailment and Epidemic Environment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5db794213c5c2da7892b","text":"A diseased environment affects breathing, produces torpor, and calls for bodily protection.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Showing, Detecting, and Verifying","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5f93e40f327f9dde88fb","text":"A concealed condition is deliberately shown, sensed by an observer, and held long enough for verification.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Gestation, Bodily Signs, and Imminent Birth","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6070912a5c52af5412e8","text":"Animal and human life proceeds through mating, conception, gestation, visible signs, birth, milk, fostering, and separation of the young.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Offering and Sanctuary Approach","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_61f5ec5882e4be9e6383","text":"A worshipper recognizes sovereignty, adopts a posture of service, and approaches through prayer, prostration, offering, and consecrated preparation.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Gift, Assignment, and Recompense","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_62bb13a9c776ac8064cb","text":"Bestowal initiates transfer, the dispatched gift gives it a recipient and route, and expected recompense makes the act reciprocal. Assignment shows that transfer can carry either favor or burden.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Greeting and Courteous Reply","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_62de3ad5aab716acdb98","text":"`ق ر ء` 96:1, 96:3; `ر ج ع` 96:8; `ك ر م` 96:3 (ٱلْأَكْرَمُ); `ر ء ي` 96:7, 96:9, 96:11, 96:13-14","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Injunction and Exhortation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_63633ed1f85f552b2b75","text":"`ك ذ ب` 96:13, 96:16 (كَذَّبَ، كَاذِبَة); `د ع و` 96:17-18; `ء م ر` 96:12; `ر ء ي` 96:7, 96:9, 96:11, 96:13-14","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Forum and Deliberative Council","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_647b1eb53c8bc06026a6","text":"The forum supplies place and participants, facing supplies reciprocal visibility, and consultation supplies the joint operation. The result is a social decision scene rather than an undifferentiated crowd.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Greeting and Courteous Reply","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6563dfd429cc95c38dd2","text":"A formulaic greeting reaches another party and is met by a returned, gracious response.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Poetic Pattern and Exchange","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_692989c45e74e999b056","text":"Speech moves between parties by attracting attention, projecting a voice, requesting uptake, and eliciting an answer.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000059/B005","root_000531/B008","root_000937/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Spirit and Apparition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6996df7315394ded2283","text":"spirit or apparition `ر ء ي:B008/m01`; human contrasted with the unseen `ء ن س:B001/m02`; apparition-like pupil image `ء ن س:B005/m03`; misleading cultic power `ط غ ي:B003/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Proportion, Appearance, and Reflection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6b44d8d14c6c23e94694","text":"Proportion gives the object a stable outline, appearance presents that outline, and reflection repeats it.
